{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"more panic","tags":["comparative"]},{"form":"most panic","tags":["superlative"]},{"form":"panick","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"adj","senses":[{"alt_of":[{"word":"Panic"}],"glosses":["Alternative letter-case form of Panic (“pertaining to the Greek god Pan”)."],"synonyms":[{"word":"Pandean"},{"tags":["obsolete"],"word":"panical"}],"tags":["Greek","alt-of","archaic"],"topics":["human-sciences","mysticism","mythology","philosophy","sciences"]},{"glosses":["Of fear, fright, etc: overwhelming or sudden."],"qualifier":"by extension (see the etymology)","synonyms":[{"tags":["obsolete"],"word":"panical"}]},{"glosses":["Pertaining to or resulting from overwhelming fear or fright."],"qualifier":"by extension (see the etymology)"}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈpænɪk/"},{"ipa":"[ˈpʰænɪk]"},{"ipa":"/ˈpeə̯nɪk/","tags":["Canada","US"]},{"ipa":"[ˈpʰeə̯nɪk] ~ /ˈpɛə̯nɪk/","tags":["Canada","US"]},{"ipa":"[ˈpʰɛə̯nɪk]","tags":["Canada","US"]},{"ipa":"/ˈpɛːnɪk/","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"[ˈpʰɛːnɪk]","tags":["Australia"]}],"upstream_line":183226,"upstream_offset":874711551,"upstream_record_sha256":"777eaabfc93157c510cc87ef08f1734ccc77c40263a74422d0b07e4a5e907b5e","word":"panic"}
{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"panics","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["Overwhelming fear or fright, often affecting groups of people or animals; (countable) an instance of this; a fright, a scare."],"tags":["uncountable"]},{"alt_of":[{"word":"kernel panic"}],"glosses":["Ellipsis of kernel panic (“on Unix-derived operating systems: an action taken by the operating system when it cannot recover from a fatal error”); (by extension) any computer system crash."],"tags":["abbreviation","alt-of","countable","ellipsis"],"topics":["computing","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A rapid reduction in asset prices due to broad efforts to raise cash in anticipation of such prices continuing to decline."],"tags":["countable"],"topics":["business","economics","finance","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A highly amusing or entertaining performer, performance, or show; a riot, a scream."],"tags":["US","colloquial","countable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈpænɪk/"},{"ipa":"[ˈpʰænɪk]"},{"ipa":"/ˈpeə̯nɪk/","tags":["Canada","US"]},{"ipa":"[ˈpʰeə̯nɪk] ~ /ˈpɛə̯nɪk/","tags":["Canada","US"]},{"ipa":"[ˈpʰɛə̯nɪk]","tags":["Canada","US"]},{"ipa":"/ˈpɛːnɪk/","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"[ˈpʰɛːnɪk]","tags":["Australia"]}],"upstream_line":183227,"upstream_offset":874723781,"upstream_record_sha256":"26eebeea3a6c4c0b8749ffaee373c523ca1ccebfcb40b6c829b22996a043658d","word":"panic"}
{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"panics","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"panicking","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"panicked","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"panicked","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To cause (someone) to feel panic (“overwhelming fear or fright”); also, to frighten (someone) into acting hastily."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To cause (a computer system) to crash."],"tags":["transitive"],"topics":["computing","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences"]},{"glosses":["To highly amuse, entertain, or impress (an audience watching a performance or show)."],"tags":["US","colloquial","transitive"]},{"glosses":["To feel panic, or overwhelming fear or fright; to freak out, to lose one's head."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["Of a computer system: to crash."],"tags":["intransitive"],"topics":["computing","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈpænɪk/"},{"ipa":"[ˈpʰænɪk]"},{"ipa":"/ˈpeə̯nɪk/","tags":["Canada","US"]},{"ipa":"[ˈpʰeə̯nɪk] ~ /ˈpɛə̯nɪk/","tags":["Canada","US"]},{"ipa":"[ˈpʰɛə̯nɪk]","tags":["Canada","US"]},{"ipa":"/ˈpɛːnɪk/","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"[ˈpʰɛːnɪk]","tags":["Australia"]}],"upstream_line":183228,"upstream_offset":874749004,"upstream_record_sha256":"ebadfda4e5b5b7cc97021c5cd587eafa408a85f51b181859d13305d99712cc47","word":"panic"}

{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"sprouts","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A new growth of or on a plant, whether from seed or other parts."]},{"glosses":["A germinated seed, an incipient young plant."],"synonyms":[{"word":"seedling"},{"word":"chit"}]},{"glosses":["A germinated seed, an incipient young plant.","An edible variety of such, grown and intended as food; examples include bean, alfalfa, kale, and others."],"synonyms":[{"word":"seedling"},{"word":"chit"}]},{"glosses":["A germinated seed, an incipient young plant.","An edible variety of such, grown and intended as food; examples include bean, alfalfa, kale, and others.","A bean sprout."],"synonyms":[{"word":"seedling"},{"word":"chit"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"Oh my, how your sprouts have grown!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A child."],"tags":["figuratively","informal"]},{"examples":[{"text":"In our family we only eat sprouts once a year, at Christmas.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A Brussels sprout."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/spɹaʊt/","tags":["US"]},{"ipa":"/spɹʌʊt/","tags":["Canada"]}],"upstream_line":169193,"upstream_offset":797030451,"upstream_record_sha256":"b0b72003d37ac29eb2dd4d61a7f6b28dcec89681ad498a51fa4e8a4c5ed106b3","word":"sprout"}
